Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a molecular_biology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 482. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 71/1.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^71(S, F) is trivial. QED.
